#### 识别依据

- Red downward arrow points to the node at the bottom center, marking it as current.
- Legend on the right identifies room types: unknown, merchant, chest, rest, enemy, elite.
- Dashed lines connect nodes vertically and diagonally across the map.
- Relics and potions read from the enlarged top inventory crop
